# Career-AI Workspace - Serena Codebase Context Map

> Generated via Serena Symbol Indexing & LSP Diagnostics

- **Project Root**: `/home/miniblues/projects/career-ai`
- **Total Crates**: `15`

## Workspace Architecture & Crate Overview

| Crate | Path | Purpose / Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `careerai-cli` | `crates/careerai-cli` | career-ai command-line interface |
| `careerai-core` | `crates/careerai-core` | Pipeline orchestration, state machine, and config for career-ai |
| `careerai-dashboard` | `crates/careerai-dashboard` | Read-only HTTP dashboard for the career-ai pipeline (KPI strip, kanban funnel, next-steps punch list) |
| `careerai-db` | `crates/careerai-db` | SQLite persistence for career-ai (sqlx models, queries, migrations) |
| `careerai-llm` | `crates/careerai-llm` | Multi-provider LLM gateway with on-disk cache |
| `careerai-match` | `crates/careerai-match` | Hard filters + keyword-similarity ranking of listings against a profile |
| `careerai-mcp` | `crates/careerai-mcp` | MCP (Model Context Protocol) server exposing the career-ai pipeline as tool calls |
| `careerai-notify` | `crates/careerai-notify` | Best-effort multi-channel notification pipeline (Slack, Telegram, email, ntfy) for career-ai |
| `careerai-pipeline` | `crates/careerai-pipeline` | Pipeline orchestration entry points (discover/match/tailor/render/apply) for career-ai |
| `careerai-profile` | `crates/careerai-profile` | Resume + LinkedIn export ingestion and canonical profile schema |
| `careerai-render` | `crates/careerai-render` | Tera → Markdown → Pandoc rendering to DOCX and ATS-safe PDF |
| `careerai-scheduler` | `crates/careerai-scheduler` | tokio-cron-scheduler daemon driving the pipeline |
| `careerai-sources` | `crates/careerai-sources` | Job-board discovery adapters behind a unified Source trait |
| `careerai-submit` | `crates/careerai-submit` | Submitters per source (HTTP, browser, email) with dry-run safety |
| `careerai-tailor` | `crates/careerai-tailor` | Constrained-diff resume tailoring and cover-letter drafting |

---
